Let's design the discrete optimizer with restricted pair-sum2 in detail.

Helper top-level functions:
1. `pad_len(n, factor=1)`: next power of two >= factor*(2n-1) maybe. For c and grad use `fft_len = next_power(3*n-2)` maybe. We can use `M = 1 << (3*n - 2).bit_length()` to compute both c and grad with same FFT length. But for c, M overkill; however simpler and maybe okay if n moderate. For n=4096, M=16384; for n=8192, M=32768; for n=6962, 3n-2=20884 next 32768. rfft of length 32768 ~ maybe 0.5ms C? Actually numpy FFT 32768 ~0.3ms? okay. 20k iterations ~6s? More in Python maybe 10-20s per 10k? Let's estimate: each iter: make x (assignment ~14k floats), rfft 32768 (~0.4ms?), square, irfft (~0.4ms), compute p, p_rev, rfft (~0.4), multiply, irfft (~0.4) total ~2ms plus Python ~0.5 => 2.5ms; 100k =>250s. Fine. For 300k =>750s. Good.

But for n=16384, M= nextpow(49150) =65536, FFT heavier ~3ms each; still maybe selected few.

2. `make_x(u, n)`: if n even: m=n//2, x[0:m]=1+u, x[m:]=1-u reversed? Need index: if n=2m, first half indices 0..m-1 = u; second half indices m..2m-1 correspond to reverse first: x[2m-1-i] =1-u_i for i=0..m-1. So x[m:] = 1 - u[::-1]. If n odd n=2m+1: indices 0..m-1=1+u, index m=1, indices m+1..2m = 1-u[::-1]. We'll store idx_left and idx_right to compute grad_u: grad_u = grad_x[left] - grad_x[right] (because x_left=1+u, x_right=1-u). For odd central no grad. Note if n even, left length m, right length m.

3. `score_from_x(x)`: S=n (since sum exactly n) if u bounds; but floating maybe sum n. Score = 2*max(conv(x))/n. We can compute max via conv; but for exact score after optimization, use full conv maybe with M=nextpow(2n-1). For internal LSE iterations we have c already.

4. `lse_eval_grad_u(u, beta, n, m, M, fft_cache?)` returns score_LSE maybe, exact cmax, grad_u. Avoid closures. We'll define a function `optimize_u_lse(u_init, n, beta, lr, steps, time_budget, start_time, best_score, best_u, do_print)` but cannot have closures; top-level can accept many args. It may create local variables but no helper inside. It must update best? Python return.

However user says all helper functions top level and no closures from function nesting; a top-level function with many args okay. Avoid nonlocal in nested; no nested functions at all. `propose_candidate` itself can contain code but no nested functions. We'll not define nested functions inside propose_candidate. It can call top-level helpers.

In `optimize_u_lse`, we need access to global t_start? Pass t_start, budget, best val via return tuple. We'll design:
```
def run_u_adam(u, n, beta, lr, steps, max_seconds, start_time, best_score, best_u):
    # returns best_score, best_u, last_u, last_score

Function `make_p_from_c(c, beta, top_k)`:
```
p = np.empty_like(c)
if beta is None:
   p.fill(0)
   k = min(top_k, len(c))
   idx = np.argpartition(-c, k-1)[:k]
   p[idx] = 1.0/k
else:
   scaled = beta*(c - c.max())
   # for numerical stability max 0
   exps = np.exp(scaled)
   z = exps.sum()
   if z ==0: p.fill(1/len)
   else p=exps/z
return p
```

```
def optimize_u(u, n, beta, lr, n_steps, max_seconds, start, best_score, best_u):
    m = n//2
    left = np.arange(0,m); right = n-1-np.arange(0,m); if odd adjust.
    x = np.empty(n)
    M=1 << ((3*n-2)-1).bit_length()  # for n? if 3n-2 <=1? n>=2.
    m = np.zeros(m); v=np.zeros(m)
    for t in range(1,steps+1):
       if time exceeded break
       # build x
       if n%2==0:
          x[:m]=1+u; x[m:]=1-u[::-1]
       else:
          x[:m]=1+u; x[m]=1; x[m+1:]=1-u[::-1]
       X=np.fft.rfft(x,M)
       c=np.fft.irfft(X*X,M)[:2*n-1]
       # exact cmax
       cmax=float(c.max())
       sc=2*cmax/n
       if sc < best_score: update
       if t%check==0 maybe exact? already have exact cmax.
       p=make_p(c,beta,top_k)
       if p is None? uniform? skip
       p_rev=p[::-1]
       P=np.fft.rfft(p_rev,M)
       y=np.fft.irfft(X*P,M)
       h=y[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
       gx=2*h/n
       gu=gx[:-?]? left/right
       ... Adam
       u=np.clip(u, -1,1)
    final check
    return
```

  rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
  deadline = time.monotonic() + budget_s - 30

  best_score=inf; best_seq=None; best_u=None; best_n=None
  # Candidate n list
  ns = []
  add 256 maybe for calib, 4096, parent_len if not too large, 8192, maybe 12288
  # Evaluate initial inits for each n and update best
  for n in ns:
    if time too low break
    candidates = []
    for amp in ...: add init_linear
    for amp... cos/cubic
    if parent: add parent projections
    if small_best exists: add interpolate small_best
    random few

Let's define `lse_u_valgrad(u, n, b, M, q)` top-level. Need not closures. It builds x, computes, returns (obj, grad). It uses numpy. For b maybe float. If `M` not passed? We can compute inside but costly; pass.

Pseudo:
```
def lse_u_valgrad(u, n, b, M):
    m=n//2
    x=np.empty(n)
    np.add(u,1.0,out=x[:m])
    if n%2: x[m]=1.0; x[m+1:]=1.0; x[m+1:]-=u[::-1]
    else: x[m:]=1.0; x[m:]-=u[::-1]
    X=np.fft.rfft(x,M)
    c=np.fft.irfft(X*X,M)[:2*n-1]
    cmax=float(c.max())
    beta=b/n
    scaled = beta*(c-cmax)
    z=np.exp(scaled).sum()
    if z==0: return cmax, zeros
    L=cmax + np.log(z)/beta
    # p = exp(scaled)/z
    # We mutated? We can compute exp again or modify c?
    exps=np.exp(scaled)
    p=exps/z
    P=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],M)
    # X currently FFT of x, still available
    y=np.fft.irfft(X*P,M)
    h=y[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    gx=2.0*h
    grad=gx[:m] - gx[-m:][::-1]
    return (L, grad)
```
Could avoid double exp by storing. Use `tmp=np.empty(2*n-1)`? But minimize calls arbitrary; allocate okay. Could preallocate? Not with variable? Not necessary.

`score_seq_raw(seq)`:
```
a=np.asarray(seq,float).ravel()
a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
S=a.sum()
if S<0.01: return inf
n=a.size
M=next_power_len(2*n-1)
X=np.fft.rfft(a,M)
c=np.fft.irfft(X*X,M)[:2*n-1]
return float(2*n*c.max()/(S*S))
```

Build x from u:
```
def build_x_u(u,n):
    m=n//2
    x=np.empty(n,float)
    np.add(u,1.0,out=x[:m])
    if n%2==0:
        x[m:]=1.0; x[m:]-=u[::-1]
    else:
        x[m]=1.0
        x[m+1:]=1.0; x[m+1:]-=u[::-1]
    return x
```
For n=1? m=0; handle: x[0]=1.0. Not needed.

`score_u` uses `build_x_u`.

`u_from_parent`: We'll implement two variants:
```
def u_from_parent(parent,n, scale=0.5):
   m=n//2
   p = parent if parent length n else interpolate to n
   # normalize to sum n? Parent length N; interpolate to n then normalize sum n
   xp=p
   if len(p)!=n: xp=np.interp(np.linspace(0,len(p)-1,n), np.linspace(0,len(p)-1,len(p)), p)
   S=xp.sum()
   if S>0: xp=xp*n/S
   left=np.arange(m)
   right=n-1-left
   d=xp[left]-xp[right]
   u=scale*d
   return np.clip(u,-1,1)
```
Maybe scale=1 preserves if diff within [-2,2]? Since xp around1, diff/2 to u? Our x=1+u, so u should be x_i-1. If xp normalized mean1, u_i≈xp_i-1, and antisym u should equal ( (xp_i-1) - (xp_j-1) )/2? If xp_j=2-xp_i, diff=xp_i-xp_j=2u, so u=0.5 diff. So scale=0.5. We'll use scale=1 variant for extra amplitude but clipped.

`interpolate_u(u, m)`: If len u !=m: return np.interp(np.linspace(0,m-1), np.linspace(0,len(u)-1), u). For m=1 fill.

`init_shape(n, amp, shape)`:
- shape 'lin': z=2*np.arange(m)/(n-1)-1; f=z
- 'sin': f=np.sin(np.pi*z/2)
- 'sin1': f=np.sin(np.pi*(2*z+1)/2)? Maybe not.
- 'cubic': f=z + q z^3 with q param.
Return amp*f. Use amp in [0,1]. For shape with values >1, clip.

`run_adam_u` implementation:
```
def run_adam_u(u, n, b, top_k, lr, max_seconds, start_time, best_score, best_u):
    m=n//2
    M=next_power_len(3*n-2)
    u=u.copy()
    mu=np.zeros(m); v=np.zeros(m)
    t=0
    while time.time? use time.monotonic - start_time < max_seconds:
       t+=1
       x=build...
       X=np.fft.rfft(x,M); c=irfft...
       cmax=c.max(); sc=2*cmax/n
       if sc<best_score: update
       p=make_p(c,b,top_k)
       if p is None or sum ~0: continue
       P=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],M); X*=P; y=irfft(X,M); h=y[...] ; gx=2*h; grad=gx[:m]-gx[-m:][::-1]
       m1=np.sqrt(t/(t+18e6))? popularity? Keep b1,b2.
       ... Adam
       u=np.clip(u,-1,1)
    final score update
    return best_score, best_u, u
```
Use `LR = lr`; Adam step normalized. Since gradient scale O(1)? For b numeric, LSE grad of L (max c) not divided by n: gx=2 h, h sum p x ≈1, so grad components O(1). Good. Step size lr ~0.02 for u. For exact top_k, same.

`make_p(c,b,top_k)`:
```
L=len(c)
if b is None:
    k=int(top_k)
    if k<=1: one-hot? Use p fill 0, p[argmax]=1; top_k=1 possible.
    else:
       p=np.zeros(L)
       if k>=L: p.fill(1/L) (bad) maybe use min(k,L-1) or threshold. For top_k<L.
       idx=np.argpartition(-c, k-1)[:k]
       p[idx]=1/k
    return p
else:
    cmax=c.max()
    scaled=b/n*(c-cmax)? But run_adam factor b is dimensionless? In valgrad b/n. Use `beta=b/n`.
    exps=np.exp(beta*(c-cmax))# max 0
    z=exps.sum()
    if z==0: p fill
    else exps/=z
    return exps
```
For top_k very large e.g., 0.25L, argpartition heavy but okay. We'll choose top_k values maybe [5,20,100,500,2000] not too large. If top_k fraction >0.5, maybe p near uniform; avoid.

`run_lbfgs_u`:
```
def run_lbfgs_u(u,n,b,M,maxfun,maxiter):
    bounds=[(-1.0,1.0)] for _ in range(n//2)
    res=minimize(lse_u_valgrad, u, args=(n,b,M), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-10,'disp':False})
    u_out=np.clip(res.x,-1,1)
    return u_out
```

beta1=0.9; beta2=0.999; eps=1e-8
m=0; v=0
for step in range(1, max+1):
   ...
   mn=beta1*mu + (1-beta1)*grad
   vn=beta2*v + (1-beta2)*grad*grad
   mhat=mn/(1-beta1**step)
   vhat=vn/(1-beta2**step)
   lr_t=lr*(1.0/(1.0+1e-4*step))
   u = u - lr_t*mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+eps)
   u=np.clip(u,-1,1)
```

right=n-1-left
d=p[left]-p[right]
u=scale*d/2? Wait if p normalized mean1 and near pair sum2, diff/2 = u. If we earlier scale parameter, define `scale` multiplies the ideal u=0.5*d. So `u=scale*0.5*d`. For scale=1 ideal. We'll do `u = 0.5 * scale * d`.
return clip.
```

    xs=np.linspace(0.0,v.size-1,n)

```python
"""
Search for low-peak self-convolution sequences.

Main idea:
- Use the reverse-antisymmetric parameterization
      x_i = 1 + h_i,   h_{N-1-i} = -h_i,   |h_i| <= 1.
  This keeps sum(x)=N, x>=0, and automatically makes reverse pairs sum to 2.
- Minimize the enhanced objective by LSE smoothing of max(self-convolution),
  using L-BFGS-B, Adam/LSE, and exact active-set subgradient Adam.
- Initialize from structured shapes, interpolated calibrated shapes, and the
  supplied parent construction (projected into the same parameterization).
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def next_power_len(length: int) -> int:
    """Smallest power of two >= length."""
    if length <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(length) - 1).bit_length()


def add_length(lengths, n):
    """Add integer length if in a sensible range and not duplicated."""
    try:
        n = int(n)
    except Exception:
        return
    if 64 <= n <= 24000 and n not in lengths:
        lengths.append(n)


def first_item(item):
    """Sort helper for lists of tuples whose first field is a score."""
    return item[0]


def build_x_u(u, n):
    """Build full sequence x = 1 + h from first-half antisymmetric part h=u."""
    n = int(n)
    m = n // 2
    x = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if u.size != m:
        u = np.interp(
            np.linspace(0.0, m - 1, m) if m > 1 else np.array([0.0]),
            np.linspace(0.0, u.size - 1, u.size) if u.size > 1 else np.array([0.0]),
            u if u.size > 1 else np.array([0.0]),
        )
        u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.clip(u, -1.0, 1.0)

    if n == 1:
        x[0] = 1.0
        return x

    np.add(u, 1.0, out=x[:m])
    if n % 2 == 0:
        x[m:] = 1.0
        x[m:] -= u[::-1]
    else:
        x[m] = 1.0
        x[m + 1:] = 1.0
        x[m + 1:] -= u[::-1]
    return x


def score_u(u, n):
    """Exact objective value for the parameterized sequence x(u)."""
    try:
        x = build_x_u(u, n)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            return np.inf
        n = int(n)
        L = 2 * n - 1
        M = next_power_len(L)
        X = np.fft.rfft(x, M)
        c = np.fft.irfft(X * X, M)[:L]
        cm = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(cm):
            return np.inf
        return float(2.0 * cm / n)
    except Exception:
        return np.inf


def score_seq_raw(seq):
    """Exact objective for an arbitrary nonnegative sequence."""
    try:
        a = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a.size == 0:
            return np.inf
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s < 0.01:
            return np.inf
        n = int(a.size)
        L = 2 * n - 1
        M = next_power_len(L)
        X = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
        c = np.fft.irfft(X * X, M)[:L]
        cm = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(cm):
            return np.inf
        return float(2.0 * n * cm / (s * s))
    except Exception:
        return np.inf


def make_p(c, b, top_k):
    """
    Construct lag weights p:
      - if b is not None: LSE weights, b is dimensionless (beta=b/n).
      - else: mean of top_k active lags.
    """
    L = c.size
    if b is None:
        k = int(np.clip(top_k, 1, L - 1))
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        if k <= 1:
            p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(-c, k - 1)[:k]
            p[idx] = 1.0 / k
        return p

    beta = float(b) / max(L, 1)  # L here is number of lags; same order as n
    cmax = float(c.max())
    scaled = beta * (c - cmax)
    p = np.exp(scaled)
    z = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(z) or z <= 0.0:
        p.fill(1.0 / L)
    else:
        p /= z
    return p


def run_adam_u(u, n, b, top_k, lr, max_seconds, start_time):
    """
    Adam on u with LSE or exact-active subgradient.
    Returns (best_score, best_u, last_u).
    """
    try:
        n = int(n)
        m = n // 2
        u = np.clip(np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), -1.0, 1.0).copy()
        if u.size != m:
            u = np.interp(
                np.linspace(0.0, m - 1, m) if m > 1 else np.array([0.0]),
                np.linspace(0.0, u.size - 1, u.size) if u.size > 1 else np.array([0.0]),
                u if u.size > 1 else np.array([0.0]),
            )
            u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
            u = np.clip(u, -1.0, 1.0)

        L = 2 * n - 1
        M = next_power_len(3 * n - 2)
        x = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)

        best_score = score_u(u, n)
        best_u = u.copy()

        mu = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        v = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        b1 = 0.9
        b2 = 0.999
        eps = 1e-8
        it = 0
        deadline = start_time + max_seconds

        while (time.monotonic() - start_time) < max_seconds:
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now > deadline:
                break
            it += 1

            u = np.clip(u, -1.0, 1.0)

            if n == 1:
                x[0] = 1.0
            else:
                np.add(u, 1.0, out=x[:m])
                if n % 2 == 0:
                    x[m:] = 1.0
                    x[m:] -= u[::-1]
                else:
                    x[m] = 1.0
                    x[m + 1:] = 1.0
                    x[m + 1:] -= u[::-1]

            X = np.fft.rfft(x, M)
            c = np.fft.irfft(X * X, M)[:L]
            cm = float(c.max())
            sc = float(2.0 * cm / n)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_u = u.copy()

            p = make_p(c, b, top_k)
            if p is None:
                break

            P = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], M)
            X *= P
            y = np.fft.irfft(X, M)
            h = y[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
            gx = 2.0 * h
            grad = gx[:m] - gx[-m:][::-1]

            if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
                break
            gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
            if gnorm <= 1e-14:
                break

            mu = b1 * mu + (1.0 - b1) * grad
            v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * (grad * grad)
            mhat = mu / (1.0 - b1 ** it)
            vhat = v / (1.0 - b2 ** it)
            lr_t = float(lr) / (1.0 + 1e-5 * it)
            u = u - lr_t * mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + eps)
            u = np.clip(u, -1.0, 1.0)

        final_sc = score_u(u, n)
        if np.isfinite(final_sc) and final_sc < best_score:
            best_score = final_sc
            best_u = u.copy()
        return best_score, best_u, u
    except Exception:
        return score_u(u, n), u, u


def lse_u_valgrad(u, n, b, M):
    """
    LSE-smoothed objective and gradient w.r.t. u.
    Objective is smoothed max(conv(x)), n is fixed, so minimizing it minimizes score.
    """
    n = int(n)
    m = n // 2
    u = np.clip(np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), -1.0, 1.0)
    x = build_x_u(u, n)
    L = 2 * n - 1

    X = np.fft.rfft(x, M)
    c = np.fft.irfft(X * X, M)[:L]
    cmax = float(c.max())

    beta = float(b) / max(n, 1)
    scaled = beta * (c - cmax)
    p = np.exp(scaled)
    z = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(z) or z <= 0.0:
        return float(cmax), np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)

    Lse = cmax + float(np.log(z)) / beta
    p /= z

    P = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], M)
    X *= P
    y = np.fft.irfft(X, M)
    h = y[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
    gx = 2.0 * h
    grad = gx[:m] - gx[-m:][::-1]
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad.fill(0.0)
    return float(Lse), grad


def run_lbfgs_u(u, n, b, maxfun=300, maxiter=300):
    """Run L-BFGS-B on the LSE objective over u in [-1, 1]."""
    try:
        n = int(n)
        m = n // 2
        u0 = np.clip(np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), -1.0, 1.0).copy()
        if u0.size != m:
            u0 = np.interp(
                np.linspace(0.0, m - 1, m) if m > 1 else np.array([0.0]),
                np.linspace(0.0, u0.size - 1, u0.size) if u0.size > 1 else np.array([0.0]),
                u0 if u0.size > 1 else np.array([0.0]),
            )
            u0 = np.clip(np.asarray(u0, dtype=np.float64), -1.0, 1.0)

        M = next_power_len(3 * n - 2)
        bounds = [(-1.0, 1.0)] * m
        try:
            res = minimize(
                lse_u_valgrad,
                u0,
                args=(n, b, M),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                    "maxfun": int(maxfun),
                    "ftol": 1e-11,
                    "gtol": 1e-9,
                    "disp": False,
                },
            )
            uout = np.clip(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64), -1.0, 1.0)
        except Exception:
            uout = u0.copy()

        return uout
    except Exception:
        return np.clip(np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64), -1.0, 1.0)


def init_u_shape(n, amp, kind, q=0.0):
    """
    Structured antisymmetric h(u) initializers.
    kind: 'lin', 'sin', 'cubic'.
    """
    n = int(n)
    m = n // 2
    if m == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    z = 2.0 * np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(n - 1, 1)) - 1.0
    if kind == "lin":
        f = z
    elif kind == "sin":
        f = np.sin(0.5 * np.pi * z)
    else:
        f = z + float(q) * (z * z * z)
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64)
    fmax = float(np.max(np.abs(f)))
    if fmax > 0.0:
        f = f / fmax
    return np.clip(float(amp) * f, -1.0, 1.0)


def interpolate_vec(v, n):
    """Interpolate a vector to length n."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if v.size == n:
        return v.copy()
    if v.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if v.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(v[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    xold = np.linspace(0.0, v.size - 1, v.size)
    xnew = np.linspace(0.0, n - 1, n)
    return np.interp(xnew, xold, v)


def u_from_parent(parent, n, scale=1.0):
    """Project an arbitrary parent sequence into antisymmetric u-space."""
    n = int(n)
    m = n // 2
    if m == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    p = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if p.size != n:
        p = interpolate_vec(p, n)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s > 0.0:
        p = p * float(n) / s
    else:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    left = np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
    right = (n - 1) - left
    d = p[left] - p[right]
    u = 0.5 * float(scale) * d
    return np.clip(u, -1.0, 1.0)


def try_update(sc, n, u, best_score, best_seq, res_score, res_u, res_n):
    """Update global/restricted best state."""
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return best_score, best_seq, res_score, res_u, res_n
    if sc < res_score:
        res_score = float(sc)
        res_u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        res_n = int(n)
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_seq = build_x_u(res_u, res_n).copy()
    return best_score, best_seq, res_score, res_u, res_n


def propose_candidate(seed=0, budget_s=2400.0):
    """
    Generate a strong sequence.  The output is a list of nonnegative floats.
    """
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t_start + float(budget_s) - 20.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if seed is not None else 0)

    # Retrieve parent construction if available.
    parent = None
    try:
        if "height_sequence_1" in globals():
            p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if p.size > 0:
                p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
                if float(p.sum()) > 0.01:
                    parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    best_score = np.inf
    best_seq = None

    if parent is not None:
        ps = score_seq_raw(parent)
        if np.isfinite(ps):
            best_score = float(ps)
            best_seq = parent.copy()
            print(f"Parent: n={parent.size}, score={ps:.10f}")

    # Lengths to evaluate for initial shapes.
    eval_ns = []
    if parent is not None:
        add_length(eval_ns, parent.size)
    for n in (512, 1024, 4096, 6144, 8192, 10240):
        add_length(eval_ns, n)

    # Primary target lengths (where we spend most optimization time).
    target_ns = []
    primary = None
    if parent is not None and 1024 <= parent.size <= 20000:
        primary = int(parent.size)
    else:
        primary = 8192
    add_length(target_ns, primary)
    add_length(target_ns, 8192)
    add_length(target_ns, 4096)
    if parent is not None:
        add_length(target_ns, int(parent.size))

    # Initial candidates: (score, n, u).
    inits = []
    for n in eval_ns:
        if (time.monotonic() - t_start) > (float(budget_s) - 300.0):
            break
        n = int(n)
        cands = []

        for amp in (0.2, 0.3, 0.4, 0.45, 0.5, 0.55, 0.6, 0.7, 0.8, 1.0):
            cands.append(init_u_shape(n, amp, "lin"))
            cands.append(init_u_shape(n, amp, "sin"))

        for amp in (0.4, 0.5, 0.6, 0.7, 0.8, 1.0):
            for q in (-0.5, -0.25, 0.0, 0.25, 0.5):
                cands.append(init_u_shape(n, amp, "cubic", q))

        if parent is not None:
            for scl in (0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0):
                cands.append(u_from_parent(parent, n, scl))

        for j in range(6):
            scale = 0.3 + 0.2 * j
            cands.append(np.clip(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n // 2), -1.0, 1.0) * scale)
            cands.append(np.clip(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0, n // 2), -1.0, 1.0) * scale)

        for u in cands:
            u = np.clip(np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64), -1.0, 1.0)
            if u.size != n // 2:
                u = interpolate_vec(u, n // 2)
            sc = score_u(u, n)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                inits.append((float(sc), n, u.copy()))

    inits.sort(key=first_item)
    best_per_n = {}
    for sc, n, u in inits:
        if list(n) not in best_per_n:
            best_per_n[n] = []
        if len(best_per_n[n]) < 8:
            best_per_n[n].append((sc, u))
        # Keep only top few structures per length.
        if len(best_per_n[n]) > 8:
            best_per_n[n].sort(key=first_item)
            best_per_n[n] = best_per_n[n][:8]

    for n in best_per_n:
        best_per_n[n].sort(key=first_item)

    # Restricted best state (even if parent is globally better, we keep it
    # as a local candidate for continuation).
    res_score = np.inf
    res_u = None
    res_n = None

    if inits:
        for sc, n, u in inits[:20]:
            best_score, best_seq, res_score, res_u, res_n = try_update(
                sc, n, u, best_score, best_seq, res_score, res_u, res_n
            )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Lightweight calibration on a small length to extract a good shape.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    cal_n = 1024
    if (deadline - time.monotonic()) > 500.0:
        add_length(eval_ns, cal_n)
        if cal_n not in best_per_n:
            cal_cands = []
            for amp in (0.4, 0.5, 0.6, 0.7, 0.8):
                cal_cands.append(init_u_shape(cal_n, amp, "sin"))
                cal_cands.append(init_u_shape(cal_n, amp, "lin"))
            for _ in range(4):
                cal_cands.append(np.clip(rng.normal(0.0, 0.8, cal_n // 2), -1.0, 1.0))
            if parent is not None:
                cal_cands.append(u_from_parent(parent, cal_n, 1.0))
            for u in cal_cands:
                sc = score_u(u, cal_n)
                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    best_per_n.setdefault(cal_n, []).append((sc, u.copy()))
            best_per_n[cal_n].sort(key=first_item)

        cal_best = None
        if cal_n in best_per_n:
            for sc, u in best_per_n[cal_n][:5]:
                if (deadline - time.monotonic()) < 80.0:
                    break
                u1 = run_lbfgs_u(u, cal_n, 30, maxfun=1200, maxiter=700)
                sc1 = score_u(u1, cal_n)
                alloc = max(5.0, min(15.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 10.0))
                _, u2, _ = run_adam_u(u1, cal_n, 300, None, 0.02, alloc, time.monotonic())
                sc2 = score_u(u2, cal_n)
                cand = (sc1, u1)
                cand2 = (sc2, u2)
                if cal_best is None or cand[0] < cal_best[0]:
                    cal_best = cand
                if cal_best is None or cand2[0] < cal_best[0]:
                    cal_best = cand2

            if cal_best is not None:
                ub = cal_best[1]
                # Insert calibrated shape into larger target lengths.
                for n in target_ns:
                    n = int(n)
                    ui = interpolate_vec(ub, n // 2)
                    sc = score_u(ui, n)
                    if np.isfinite(sc):
                        best_per_n.setdefault(n, []).append((sc, ui.copy()))
                        best_per_n[n].sort(key=first_item)
                        if len(best_per_n[n]) > 8:
                            best_per_n[n] = best_per_n[n][:8]
                        best_score, best_seq, res_score, res_u, res_n = try_update(
                            sc, n, ui, best_score, best_seq, res_score, res_u, res_n
                        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Main optimization on target lengths.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if target_ns:
        # Primary first, then by current best known score.
        target_ns_sorted = []
        for n in target_ns:
            if n == primary:
                continue
            if n in best_per_n:
                target_ns_sorted.append((best_per_n[n][0], n))
            else:
                target_ns_sorted.append((10.0, n))
        target_ns_sorted.sort(key=first_item)
        target_order = [primary] + [n for _, n in target_ns_sorted]

        for idx, n in enumerate(target_order):
            n = int(n)
            if (deadline - time.monotonic()) < 120.0:
                break
            if n not in best_per_n:
                best_per_n[n] = []
            if not best_per_n[n]:
                u0 = init_u_shape(n, 0.55, "sin")
                sc0 = score_u(u0, n)
                if np.isfinite(sc0):
                    best_per_n[n].append((sc0, u0.copy()))

            if not best_per_n[n]:
                continue

            priority = 1.0 if idx == 0 else (0.35 if idx == 1 else 0.12)

            branch_list = best_per_n[n][:3]
            print(f"\nOptimizing target n={n}, priority={priority:.2f}, "
                  f"best init={branch_list[0][0]:.10f}, remaining={deadline-time.monotonic():.0f}s")

            for b_idx, (sc0, u0) in enumerate(branch_list):
                if (deadline - time.monotonic()) < 120.0:
                    break
                if b_idx > 0 and (deadline - time.monotonic()) < 300.0:
                    break

                lbf_b = [10, 50, 200, 700, 2000, 6000]
                if n >= 8000:
                    maxfun_per_b = 180
                    maxiter_per_b = 180
                elif n >= 2048:
                    maxfun_per_b = 320
                    maxiter_per_b = 320
                else:
                    maxfun_per_b = 900
                    maxiter_per_b = 600

                branch_best_sc = float(sc0)
                branch_best_u = np.asarray(u0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                u_cur = branch_best_u.copy()

                for b in lbf_b:
                    if (deadline - time.monotonic()) < 90.0:
                        break
                    had_scale = 1.0 if n >= 8000 else (0.7 if n >= 2048 else 1.0)
                    mf = int(maxfun_per_b * had_scale)
                    u_new = run_lbfgs_u(u_cur, n, b, maxfun=mf, maxiter=mf)
                    sc = score_u(u_new, n)
                    best_score, best_seq, res_score, res_u, res_n = try_update(
                        sc, n, u_new, best_score, best_seq, res_score, res_u, res_n
                    )

                    if np.isfinite(sc) and (sc < branch_best_sc - 1e-13 or sc < branch_best_sc * 1.001):
                        if sc < branch_best_sc:
                            branch_best_sc = sc
                            branch_best_u = u_new.copy()
                        u_cur = u_new.copy()
                    else:
                        # Do not jump far downhill in exact score.
                        u_cur = branch_best_u.copy()

                # Adam LSE polish: smooth then sharp.
                rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
                if rem > 120.0:
                    alloc1 = float(min(700.0 * priority, 0.45 * rem))
                    if alloc1 > 30.0:
                        _, u_a, _ = run_adam_u(branch_best_u, n, 60, None, 0.020,
                                               alloc1, time.monotonic())
                        sc = score_u(u_a, n)
                        best_score, best_seq, res_score, res_u, res_n = try_update(
                            sc, n, u_a, best_score, best_seq, res_score, res_u, res_n
                        )
                        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < branch_best_sc:
                            branch_best_sc = sc
                            branch_best_u = u_a.copy()

                    if (deadline - time.monotonic()) - time.monotonic() > 0:
                        rem2 = deadline - time.monotonic()
                        alloc2 = float(min(600.0 * priority, 0.30 * rem2))
                        if alloc2 > 30.0:
                            _, u_a2, _ = run_adam_u(branch_best_u, n, 1000, None, 0.010,
                                                    alloc2, time.monotonic())
                            sc2 = score_u(u_a2, n)
                            best_score, best_seq, res_score, res_u, res_n = try_update(
                                sc2, n, u_a2, best_score, best_seq, res_score, res_u, res_n
                            )
                            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < branch_best_sc:
                                branch_best_sc = sc2
                                branch_best_u = u_a2.copy()

                # Exact active-set subgradient Adam.
                rem3 = deadline - time.monotonic()
                if rem3 > 90.0 and (deadline - time.monotonic()) > 90.0:
                    total_topk = float(min(500.0 * priority, 0.30 * rem3))
                    topks = [5, 30, 150, 800]
                    each = total_topk / float(len(topks))
                    u_top = branch_best_u.copy()
                    for topk in topks:
                        if (deadline - time.monotonic()) < 45.0:
                            break
                        _, u_t, _ = run_adam_u(u_top, n, None, topk, 0.007,
                                               each, time.monotonic())
                        sc = score_u(u_t, n)
                        u_top2 = u_t.copy()
                        best_score, best_seq, res_score, res_u, res_n = try_update(
                            sc, n, u_t2, best_score, best_seq, res_score, res_u, res_n
                        )
                        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < branch_best_sc:
                            branch_best_sc = sc
                            branch_best_u = u_t2.copy()
                        u_top = u_t2 if np.isfinite(sc) else u_top

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final local escape attempts from best restricted state.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if res_u is not None and (deadline - time.monotonic()) > 180.0:
        n0 = int(res_n)
        u0 = np.asarray(res_u, dtype=np.float64).copy()

        # Small noise restarts.
        for nscale in (0.04, 0.08, 0.15):
            if (deadline - time.monotonic()) < 80.0:
                break
            u_noisy = np.clip(u0 + rng.normal(0.0, nscale, len(u0)), -1.0, 1.0)
            u1 = run_lbfgs_u(u_noisy, n0, 200, maxfun=220, maxiter=220)
            sc1 = score_u(u1, n0)
            best_score, best_seq, res_score, res_u, res_n = try_update(
                sc1, n0, u1, best_score, best_seq, res_score, res_u, res_n
            )
            if (deadline - time.monotonic()) < 50.0:
                break
            _, u2, _ = run_adam_u(u1, n0, 600, None, 0.010,
                                  min(60.0, max(10.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 30.0)),
                                  time.monotonic())
            sc2 = score_u(u2, n0)
            best_score, best_seq, res_score, res_u, res_n = try_update(
                sc2, n0, u2, best_score, best_seq, res_score, res_u, res_n
            )

        # Slight amplitude rescaling can help rounding around boundaries.
        for amp in (0.85, 0.95, 1.10):
            if (deadline - time.monotonic()) < 60.0:
                break
            u_scaled = np.clip(float(amp) * u0, -1.0, 1.0)
            u3 = run_lbfgs_u(u_scaled, res_n, 400, maxfun=180, maxiter=180)
            sc3 = score_u(u3, int(res_n))
            best_score, best_seq, res_score, res_u, res_n = try_update(
                sc3, int(res_n), u3, best_score, best_seq, res_score, res_u, res_n
            )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Fallback and final validation.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if best_seq is None:
        if res_u is not None:
            best_seq = build_x_u(res_u, int(res_n)).copy()
            best_score = res_score
        else:
            best_seq = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
            best_score = score_seq_raw(best_seq)

    out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, float(x)))) for x in np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64)]
    if sum(out) < 0.01:
        out = [1.0] * 64

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = float("inf")

    if not np.isfinite(verified) or verified >= float(best_score):
        # If internal score is better than the external validator report
        # (e.g. due to a transient local bug), still try to return the better
        # validated parent if available.
        if parent is not None:
            out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, float(x)))) for x in parent]
            try:
                verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
            except Exception:
                verified = float("inf")

    print("\n" + "=" * 64)
    print(f"FINAL: internal={float(best_score):.10f}, verified={verified:.10f}")
    print(f"Length: {len(out)}, Time: {time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s / {budget_s}s")
    print("=" * 64)
    return out
```
